## Teapot Pilgrim / 1 star
SUPPORT WHILE OWNED · offered 1-star body
{bonus:Heal|Champion / Healing per second|+0.04}
{bonus:MoveSpeed|Champion / Movement speed|-0.3%}
TALENT · Each DRINK purchased during this run adds 4% to this ally's weapon damage, up to +40%. Applies when the next battle is prepared; not a team-wide damage buff.
PASSIVE · Every 4s in combat, attempt to heal the hero for 1.5 HP before healing modifiers. Healing restrictions apply.
Immortal · 0 matching 1-star bodies owned. Three matching bodies merge; one of their weapons is kept.
Shown support is per body, not the purchase delta; merging replaces its ingredients.
Random weapon: displayed attack ranges use your current build before this purchase. Melee and ranged weapons are eligible.
“I dropped a cup. I have never dropped a cup.”

LIFE IN THE WAITING REALM
Born above the station kitchens, they fed travellers through a winter with no departures. Every cup was counted; nobody knew whether they had eaten. They want a table where the last person serving is also given a seat.

SOURCE
Regular shop; hero theme and unlock wave apply.
Named talents count bodies and absorbed memories; star level only scales the separately listed support unless stated.

WEAPON PREVIEW / CURRENT BUILD
53 eligible random weapons. Damage 4.08–46.61; cycle 0.52–1.87s; reach 2.06–7.63. Bounds use the current build before recruiting. Different weapons produce different attacks; these ranges are not guaranteed together. Purchase talents and merges can change the final build.

Hearth / Champion: {bonus:Heal|Champion / Healing per second|+0.135} every 2 bodies. Card shows cumulative reward at the next threshold. Extra at 4 bodies: {bonus:Heal|Champion / Healing per second|+0.135}. Extra at 6 bodies: {bonus:Heal|Champion / Healing per second|+0.27}.

Masked Carnival / Run: {bonus:ChestQuality|Run / Chest quality|+9%} every 3 bodies. Card shows cumulative reward at the next threshold.

Archive / Team: {bonus:Damage|Team / Damage|+7.2%} every 2 bodies. Card shows cumulative reward at the next threshold. Extra at 4 bodies: {bonus:Damage|Team / Damage|+7.2%}. Extra at 6 bodies: {bonus:Damage|Team / Damage|+14.4%}.

Lantern Guild / Team: {bonus:AttackRange|Team / Attack range|+9%} every 2 bodies. Card shows cumulative reward at the next threshold.
